Potential issue: For n=6121, direct np.convolve O(n^2) in evaluate is heavy if we call many times. But we can use our own FFT objective for search and call evaluate_sequence only for final or occasional. Is that allowed? Yes, you can use libraries and call evaluate as many times; using own evaluator should be fine, but need ensure not exploiting rounding. We can return best from own computation then maybe evaluate once to confirm. But if our own has slight errors, best maybe not actually as good. Detailed algorithm proposal:
- Top-level helpers:
  - fast_conv(a): np.fft.rfft next fast len, square, irfft, trim. For nonnegative a but FFT may produce tiny negative; take max(np.max(c), 0) maybe. To get c for objective, use real. Use scipy.signal.next_fast_len? If scipy available. Could implement next_power_of_two for simplicity: size = 1; while size < 2n-1: size <<=1. But for n=8192, size=16384, good. Use np.fft.rfft with nsize. Square: spec=fft(a); conv=np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, size)[:2n-1]. This computes linear convolution? np.fft convolution with zero padding length ≥ n+n-1. Good. For nonnegative, max should be positive. Slight errors; we can set c = np.maximum(c,0) for max? But true coefficients nonnegative. If FFT error yields negative tiny, max unaffected (unless all tiny negative? impossible). For gradient with λ, use same.
  - score_fast(a): S=np.sum(a); c=conv(a); m=np.max(c); if m<=0 or S<... return 0; return (S*S)/m. Objective=2*n/score.
  - Maybe use np.float64. For very large values, overflow? We can normalize sequence for fast computations to sum=1 to avoid overflow. Since score scale invariant. For exact evaluate, it clamps [0,1000], but sum can be small? If we return normalized sum=1, fine. Our internal extend maybe sum not exactly 1 but ok. Keep nonnegative.
- Initial sequences:
  1. power one-sided: a[i] = (i+0.5)^(-0.5) normalized.
  2. generalized beta one-sided: a[i] = (i+1)^-p * (n-i)^-q, p,q in [0,0.5]. But if q near 0.5 both singular, convolution peak might high; but we can sample random (p,q), choose top by fast score.
  3. exponential tilt: base one-sided * exp(beta*i/n), beta in [-1,1]. Normalized.
  4. trigonometric/Gaussian-like? Could random smooth functions: a=exp(Σ coeff cos?) positive. But need good.
  5. Recurrence method: Given target c for early k, solve a from c. We can set target c_k = M * shape d(k/n) for k=0..n-1; recurrence a_k = (target_k - ∑_{i=1}^{min(k,n-1)} a_i a_{k-i} - diag?) / a_0. Need choose a0. Since scale invariant, set a0=1 and compute a recursively for k=1..n-1. This ensures first n coefficients equal target? Actually c_k for k=0..n-1 depend on a_0..a_k, so if we set c_k = d_k for k=0..n-1, recurrence determines a. Need a0=√d0. For k≥1: c_k=2∑_{i=0}^{floor((k-1)/2)} a_i a_{k-i} + (a_{k/2}^2 if even). Solve for a_k: c_k = 2∑_{i=0}^{floor((k-1)/2)} a_i a_{k-i} + (if k even a_k^2? Wait term i=k/2: a_k a_{k/2}=a_k^2 if even. Let's derive: c_k = a_k a_0 + 2∑_{i=1}^{floor((k-1)/2)} a_i a_{k-i} + (if k even a_{k/2}^2). Actually a_k appears only paired with a0 (if k<n) and if k even? a_k is at i=k, j=0, and i=0,j=k; both are same term counted twice? In convolution c_k=∑_{i=0}^k a_i a_{k-i}; includes i=k => a_k a_0 and i=0 => a_0 a_k, so 2a_k a_0. No a_k^2 term unless k=0. For k even, i=k/2 gives a_{k/2}^2, not a_k. Thus a_k = (c_k - [2∑_{i=1}^{floor((k-1)/2)?} + diag?])/(2a0). More exactly: c_k = 2a_0 a_k + ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}. For i from 1 to k-1; correct. So a_k=(c_k - ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i})/(2a0). This is O(n^2) if naive; n=2048 maybe okay; n=8192 O(67M) in Python too slow unless vectorized. But we only need a few initial designs; O(n^2) for 2048 okay? Could use FFT to solve recurrence? Recurrence for c is convolution; maybe iterative deconvolution: We want A^2 = D_trunc? But if we only match early n, high terms determined. Could find A=sqrt(D) if D polynomial length 2n-1 and target has flat? Not.
   - But recurrence O(n^2) can be avoided: If we choose target c_k = M for k=0..n-1 (constant), recurrence gives one-sided square root (binomial) O(n^2?) but direct formula base_power. If target d shape, e.g., d_k=1 - β(k/n)^p, we can compute recurrence using direct sums O(n^2); for n=4096 O16M per target maybe OK in Python loops? Too slow. Potential optimization algorithms:
A. Parameterized family optimizations using scipy.optimize (bounded) on low-dimensional parameters for several families, with fast FFT score. This can find good continuous shapes without full nonconvex full-sequence. We can then use full sequence local search to refine.

Families:

Known algorithm:
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n+1) > css[:-1]
    rho = np.max(np.where(cond)[0]) + 1
    theta = (css[rho-1] - 1) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)

Helper:
def auto_conv_fitted(u, n, size):
    fu=np.fft.rfft(u, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fu*fu, size)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
def cross_corr_fitted(u, w, n, size):
    ur=u[::-1]
    fu=np.fft.rfft(ur, size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fu*fw, size)
    t=r[n-1:2*n-1]
    return t
Careful: w length 2n-1, u length n. For cross, size ≥ n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. If size too small wrap.
Softmax:
def soft_max_weights(b, tau):
    bmax=np.max(b)
    ex=np.exp((b-bmax)/tau)
    s=np.sum(ex)
    w=ex/s
    soft=bmax + tau*np.log(s)
    return soft,w
Since b sum? not needed.
Adaptive tau: For gradual, use list. For final near crisp, tau small. But if tau too small and max plateau, gradient may be noisy; use crisp line search.

Adam implementation:
def adam_step(params, grad, state, lr, t):
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
    mhat=m/(1-beta1**t)
    vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
    step=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    return params-step, updated state
But for full log, we need state arrays. Since top-level no closures, can store in local variables in propose; helper function can return updated. Define top-level `adam_update(params, grad, m, v, t, lr)`.

Smooth noise function:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma):
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    # random low freq cos/sin
    for m in range(1,9):
        amp=sigma/(m**1.5)*np.sqrt(1.0/n?)? We want noise std sigma. Use total variance sum amp^2/2 ≈ sigma^2? Simpler: generate white, then smooth spectral. But simple:
    f=np.fft.rfft(rng.normal(0,1,n))
    L=2*n-1? no for noise length n. Use size n.
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    cutoff=1.0
    mask=np.exp(-(freq/cutoff)**2)
    noise=np.fft.irfft(f*mask, n)
    noise /= (np.std(noise)+1e-12)
    return sigma*noise
```
This uses FFT; okay. But initial many noises; maybe simple random cos:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, sigma, terms=8):
    t=np.arange(n)/n
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, terms+1):
        phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        amp=sigma/(m*0.5)
        noise += amp*np.cos(np.pi*m*t+phase)
    # scale to std sigma
    noise *= sigma/(np.std(noise)+1e-12)
    return noise
```
Use terms 10.

Let's define Adam helper with optional lr. For full, state arrays length n. For param, length D.
```
def adam_update(params, grad, m, v, step, lr, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
    bc1 = 1 - beta1**step
    bc2 = 1 - beta2**step
    params = params - lr * (m/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v/bc2)+eps)
    return params,m,v
```
But if grad has zeros, m decays. Good.

Parameter optimizer can use same helper. Need avoid `**` for arrays? scalar beta1**step okay.

Function to convert theta to u:
```
def theta_to_u(theta, B, n):
    L = B.dot(theta)
    Lmax=np.max(L)
    L = L - Lmax
    u=np.exp(L)
    s=u.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: fallback uniform
    return u/s
```
B includes -logx,-log1x, cos/sin. Since L arbitrary offset, softmax normalized.

Algorithm "spectral sqrt projection (PSP)":

This is like solving A^2=D. If D flat, B would not be real nonnegative, but projection may find something. The sqrt step can introduce oscillatory negative coefficients; projection to nonnegative smooths? Could be useful as a metaheuristic.

Procedure:
```
def spectral_sqrt_search(a, n, N, deadline, best_u, best_R):
    a_full = pad a to N
    cnt=0
    # choose target mixing with flat based on current C
    rho=0.1
    while time:
        C = auto_conv_cyclic(a_full,N)
        C = np.maximum(C,0)
        S2 = np.sum(C) # = (sum a)^2 if cyclic no wrap? if support N, sum same.
        # target D: normalize C to have sum=S2 to keep scale, but target average = S2/N. If we want lower max, D should have max lower than C? We can cap C:
        D = C
        cap = max(0.5*np.max(C), S2/N) maybe?
        D = np.minimum(C, cap)
        D = 0.5*D + 0.5*(S2/N)*np.ones(N)  # mix with flat
        # preserve sum S2? Mix has same sum? D sum may change. Scale D to sum S2.
        D *= S2 / max(sum(D),1e-12)
        D = np.maximum(D,0)
        H = np.fft.rfft(D,N)
        # sqrt with branch
        S = np.sqrt(H)  # complex sqrt; ensure H[0] real positive
        # Because rfft, for m none? Need construct full? Use rfft length N; irfft of S works if S satisfies Hermitian? principal sqrt of rfft values for non-Hermitian? For real D, rfft[0], rfft[N/2] real, interior rfft toggles Hermitian if input real; sqrt principal should preserve conjugate symmetry for DFT of real? Since H[k+1] maybe not in rfft? rfft contains first N/2+1; conjugate symmetric. Principal sqrt of conjugate pairs is conjugate (angles in [-π,π]), yes. So B_full = irfft(S,N) real.
        B = np.real(B_full)
        # enforce support first n? Also maybe symmetrize? We want length n; last n should ideally zero. Project last n to zero.
        B = B[:n].copy()
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        s = B.sum()
        if s>0: B/=s
        # blend
        a = (1-0.2)*a + 0.2*B; normalize
        C=conv; R fast; update best
        # ramp rho? maybe cap fraction 0.5..0.7
```

```
def spectral_cap_search(u,n,N,L,deadline):
    a=u
    best=a,bestR
    cap_factor=0.95
    smooth=0.3
    while time:
       C=conv_cyclic(a,N)[:L]
       C=max(C,0); Csum=C.sum()
       M=np.max(C)
       R=2*n*M
       update best
       target=np.minimum(C, cap_factor*M)
       # mix with flat? Maybe target = (1-s)*target + s*flat_avg
       target = (1-smooth_mix)*target + smooth_mix*(Csum/L)*np.ones(L)
       target *= Csum / target.sum()
       # extend zeros
       D=np.zeros(N); D[:L]=target
       H=rfft(D,N)
       S=principal_sqrt(H)

Full function returns best. It may use `lowpass_noise` for kicks. That function uses `np.fft` and loops terms? We can implement simple:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, sigma, cutoff=3.0):
    f=np.fft.rfft(rng.normal(0,1,n))
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    mask=np.exp(-(freq*cutoff)**2)
    noise=np.fft.irfft(f*mask,n)
    sd=noise.std()
    if sd>1e-15: noise *= sigma/sd
    return noise
```
One FFT per kick, okay. cutoff 3 means features ~ few wavelengths. sigma 0.05.

`normalize_u`:
```
def normalize_u(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[a<0]=0.0
    s=np.sum(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    a *= 1.0/s
    return a
```
`make_exact_dec(n)`:
```
a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
a[0]=1.0
for i in range(n-1):
    a[i+1]=a[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
return normalize_u(a)
```

```
def generate_initials(n, rng):
    dec=make_exact_dec(n)
    cands=[dec]
    inc=dec[::-1].copy()
    cands.append(inc)
    for mix in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9]:
       u=normalize_u((1-mix)*dec + mix*inc)
       cands.append(u)
    # powers
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)+0.5
    for p in [0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6]:
       u=normalize_u(idx**(-p))
       cands.append(u)
    # smooth perturb dec/inc
    for sigma in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
       L=np.log(dec)+lowpass_noise(n,rng,sigma)
       cands.append(softmax_from_log(L))
       L=np.log(inc)+lowpass_noise(n,rng,sigma)
       cands.append(softmax_from_log(L))
    return cands
```

def linear_conv_max_scaled(u,n,size1):
    fu=np.fft.rfft(u,size1)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fu*fu,size1)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c*n, np.max(c*n)
```

Detailed functions:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    p=1
    while p<m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```

`lowpass_noise` uses rng.normal; if rng is None? Pass.
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, sigma, cutoff=3.0):
    white = rng.standard_normal(n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(white)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    mask = np.exp(-(freq * cutoff) ** 2)
    noise = np.fft.irfft(f * mask, n)
    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if sd > 1e-15:
        noise *= sigma / sd
    else:
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    return noise
```
Cutoff=3 means wavelengths ~ n/3; smooth enough. Could make cutoff=5 for smoother.

`softmax_from_log(L)`:
```
def _softmax_from_log(L): # no underscore? okay top-level
    L = np.asarray(L, dtype=np.float64)
    Lmax = np.max(L)
    if not np.isfinite(Lmax): Lmax=0
    Ls = L - Lmax
    Ls = np.clip(Ls, -70.0, 0.0)
    u = np.exp(Ls)
    s = u.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones_like(L)/L.size
    return u/s
```

`compute_b_scaled(a,n,size1)`:
```
def compute_b_scaled(a, n, size1):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size1)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size1)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    b=c*float(n)
    return b, float(np.max(b))
```
If a not length n? It is. If a sum not1, b scaling n but objective? We always normalize. For initial candidate maybe normalized. Good.

`soft_max_weights`:
```
def soft_max_weights(b, tau):
    tau=max(float(tau),1e-12)
    bmax=float(np.max(b))
    ex=np.exp((b-bmax)/tau)
    s=float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(b); w[np.argmax(b)]=1; return bmax,w
    w=ex/s
    soft=bmax+tau*np.log(s)
    return float(soft), w
```
When tau small and b range maybe >1, ex underflows except max; s=1. Good.

`cross_grad`:
```
def cross_grad_scaled(u,w,n,size2):
    ur=u[::-1]
    fu=np.fft.rfft(ur,size2)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size2)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fu*fw,size2)
    t=r[n-1:2*n-1]
    g=2.0*float(n)*t
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    return g
```
Could `size2` not big enough if u contains negative? no.

`adam_update`:
```
def adam_update(p, g, m, v, t, lr, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
    t=f+1
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
    bc1=1.0 - b1**t
    bc2=1.0 - b2**t
    step = lr * (m/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v/bc2)+eps)
    p = p - step
    return p,m,v,t
```
Potential overflow if g huge. If nonfinite, reset. Use `if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)): p = p; m=m*0; v=v*0; t=t; continue`. Need check in caller.

`make_basis(n,M)`:
```
def make_basis(n, M):
    x=(np.arange(n,dtype=float)+0.5)/n
    eps=1.0/(2.0*n)
    logx=np.log(x+eps)
    log1x=np.log(1.0-x+eps)
    rows=[-logx, -log1x]
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       args=np.pi*m*x
       rows.append(np.cos(args))
       rows.append(np.sin(args))
    return np.vstack(rows)
```
Memory okay.

`run_spectral` full code:
```
def run_spectral(u_in, n, deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1):
    a=normalize_u(u_in.copy())
    N=next_pow2(2*n)
    L=2*n-1
    start=time.monotonic()
    b,maxb=compute_b_scaled(a,n,size1)
    R=2*maxb
    if R<best_R: best_R=R; best_u=a.copy()
    no_improve=0
    total_budget=max(1.0, deadline-start)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic()-start
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed/total_budget)
        # recompute b for current a (unless already from start? loop start enough; after update, next loop)
        b, maxb = compute_b_scaled(a,n,size1)
        R=2.0*maxb
        if R < best_R:
            best_R=R; best_u=a.copy(); no_improve=0
            print(f"spectral R={R:.6f} t={elapsed:.1f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        avg = float(n)/float(L)
        cap_frac = 0.96 - 0.24*progress
        if no_improve > 500:
            cap_frac = max(0.70, cap_frac - 0.05)
        mix_frac = 0.15 + 0.35*progress
        alpha = 0.35 - 0.20*progress
        # target max: try just below current best_M and below cap max
        target_M = max(avg, min(maxb*cap_frac, (best_R/2.0) - 0.003 - 0.005*progress))
        D = np.minimum(b, target_M)
        D = (1.0-mix_frac)*D + mix_frac*avg
        D = D + 1e-9
        sD=np.sum(D)
        if sD>0 and np.isfinite(sD): D *= float(n)/sD
        Dfull=np.zeros(N)
        Dfull[:L]=D
        H=np.fft.rfft(Dfull,N)
        H[0]=abs(H[0])
        S=np.sqrt(H.astype(np.complex128))
        if N%2==0:
           S[N//2]=complex(np.sqrt(abs(S[N//2].real)),0.0)
        if no_improve > 400:
           kmax=max(1,N//4)
           kmin=1
           kk=rng.integers(kmin, N//2, size=kmax)
           S[kk]*=-1
        B=np.fft.irfft(S,N)
        B=np.real(B[:n])
        Bp=np.maximum(B,0.0)
        sB=Bp.sum()
        if sB>1e-15:
           Bp/=sB
           a=normalize_u((1-alpha)*a + alpha*Bp)
    return best_u,best_R
```
What if target_M > maxb, D=b, mix with flat; okay. If target_M<avg, max ensures avg. If best_R/2 - offset < avg, target avg. At low best_R, target flat, may push uniform. okay.
Print maybe too frequent; only on improve. Could every 500. Use count.

def run_full(u_in,n,deadline,best_u,best_R,rng,size1,size2):

Double-check `safe_adam_update`:
```
def safe_adam_update(p,g,m,v,t,lr,b1=0.9,b2=0.999,eps=1e-8):
    t1=t+1
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
    bc1=1-b1**t1; bc2=1-b2**t1
    step=lr*(m/bc1)/(np.sqrt(v/bc2)+eps)
    p_new=p-step
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_new)):
       return p, np.zeros_like(m), np.zeros_like(v), t, False
    return p_new,m,v,t1,True
```
If g contains nan, m/v nan; check p_new. Should also if not finite g: return p with reset. Add at start:

if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
   return p, np.zeros_like(m), np.zeros_like(v), t, False
```

if not (np.all(np.isfinite(m)) and np.all(np.isfinite(v))): return p, zeros, zeros, t, False
```

```python
"""
Search with hybrid spectral / smooth-gradient optimization.

The objective is scale invariant and, for normalized nonnegative a,
  R = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)).

The code:
 1. Builds many analytic/random initial sequences, including the exact
    one-sided square-root sequence of (1-x)^(-1/2).
 2. Runs a fast spectral alternating-inverse-square-root projection
    to aggressively lower the largest convolution coefficient.
 3. Runs a low-dimensional smooth log-spline optimizer.
 4. Runs a full-sequence Adam optimizer in log-space with annealed
    soft-max approximations of the max coefficient.
Finally it evaluates a handful of surviving candidates with the official
evaluate_sequence() oracle and returns the best official one.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_u(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a *= 1.0 / s
    return a


def softmax_from_log(L):
    L = np.asarray(L, dtype=np.float64)
    Lmax = float(np.max(L))
    if not np.isfinite(Lmax):
        Lmax = 0.0
    Ls = L - Lmax
    Ls = np.clip(Ls, -70.0, 0.0)
    u = np.exp(Ls)
    s = float(np.sum(u))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L.size)
    return u / s


def lowpass_noise(n, rng, sigma, cutoff=3.0):
    white = rng.standard_normal(n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(white)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    mask = np.exp(-(freq * cutoff) ** 2)
    noise = np.fft.irfft(f * mask, n)
    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if sd > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(sd):
        noise *= sigma / sd
    else:
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    return noise


def make_exact_dec(n):
    """First n coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2), normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2 * (i - 1) + 1) / (2 * i)
    return normalize_u(a)


def make_basis(n, M):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1.0 / (2.0 * float(n))
    logx = np.log(x + eps)
    log1x = np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    rows = [-logx, -log1x]
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        t = np.pi * float(m) * x
        rows.append(np.cos(t))
        rows.append(np.sin(t))
    return np.vstack(rows)


def generate_initials(n, rng):
    dec = make_exact_dec(n)
    inc = dec[::-1].copy()
    cands = [dec.copy(), inc.copy(), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    for mix in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
        cands.append(normalize_u((1.0 - mix) * dec + mix * inc))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    for p in (0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.65):
        vals = idx ** (-p)
        cands.append(normalize_u(vals))

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    for sigma, w in ((0.02, 1.0), (0.05, 0.7), (0.08, 0.4), (0.12, 0.0)):
        L = -0.5 * np.log(x + 1e-7)
        L += sigma * lowpass_noise(n, rng, 1.0, cutoff=6.0)
        L += w * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) * 0.1
        cands.append(softmax_from_log(L))

        L = -0.5 * np.log(1.0 - x + 1e-7)
        L += sigma * lowpass_noise(n, rng, 1.0, cutoff=6.0)
        L += w * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) * 0.1
        cands.append(softmax_from_log(L))

    return cands


def compute_b_scaled(a, n, size1):
    """Return b = n*conv(a,a), trimmed to length 2n-1."""
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size1)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size1)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    b = c * float(n)
    return b, float(np.max(b))


def soft_max_weights(b, tau):
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-12)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    ex = np.exp((b - bmax) / tau)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return bmax, w
    w = ex / s
    soft = bmax + tau * float(np.log(s))
    return soft, w


def cross_grad_scaled(u, w, n, size2):
    """
    Gradient factor for b = n*conv(u,u) with weights w.
    Returns g_i = 2*n*sum_j u_j w_{i+j}.
    """
    ur = u[::-1]
    fu = np.fft.rfft(ur, size2)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size2)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fu * fw, size2)
    t = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = 2.0 * float(n) * t
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def safe_adam_update(p, g, m, v, t, lr, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return p, np.zeros_like(m), np.zeros_like(v), t, False

    t1 = t + 1
    m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
    v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)

    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(m)) and np.all(np.isfinite(v))):
        return p, np.zeros_like(m), np.zeros_like(v), t, False

    bc1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t1
    bc2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t1
    step = lr * (m / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v / bc2) + eps)
    p_new = p - step

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_new)):
        return p, np.zeros_like(m), np.zeros_like(v), t, False

    return p_new, m, v, t1, True


def run_spectral(u_in, n, deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1):
    a = normalize_u(u_in.copy())
    N = next_pow2(2 * n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    avg = float(n) / float(L)

    start = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    print_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        total_budget = max(1.0, float(deadline - start))
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / total_budget)

        b, maxb = compute_b_scaled(a, n, size1)
        R = 2.0 * maxb

        if R < best_R:
            best_R = R
            best_u = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            print_count += 1
            if print_count % 20 == 1:
                print(f"  spectral improve R={R:.6f} at t={elapsed:.1f}s")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        cap_frac = 0.96 - 0.26 * progress
        if no_improve > 500:
            cap_frac = max(0.68, cap_frac - 0.06)

        mix_frac = 0.18 + 0.34 * progress
        alpha = 0.36 - 0.22 * progress

        # Try to cap the current peak slightly below the best peak.
        target_M = max(avg, min(maxb * cap_frac, (best_R / 2.0) - 0.003 - 0.018 * progress))

        D = np.minimum(b, target_M)
        D = (1.0 - mix_frac) * D + mix_frac * avg
        D = D + 1e-9
        sD = float(np.sum(D))
        if sD > 0 and np.isfinite(sD):
            D *= float(n) / sD

        Dfull = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        Dfull[:L] = D

        H = np.fft.rfft(Dfull, N)
        H[0] = abs(H[0])
        S = np.sqrt(H.astype(np.complex128))
        if N % 2 == 0:
            S[N // 2] = complex(np.sqrt(abs(S[N // 2].real)), 0.0)

        # Random phase flips only when stuck, to escape symmetric square roots.
        if no_improve > 350 and int(no_improve % 7) == 0:
            kmax = max(1, N // 5)
            kk = rng.integers(1, max(2, N // 2), size=kmax)
            S[kk] *= -1.0

        Bfull = np.fft.irfft(S, N)
        B = np.real(Bfull[:n])
        Bp = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        sB = float(np.sum(Bp))
        if sB > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(sB):
            Bp *= 1.0 / sB
            a = normalize_u((1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * Bp)

    return best_u, best_R


def run_parameter(n, basis, deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1, size2):
    D = basis.shape[0]
    theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[0] = 0.5
    # Small random smooth part for diversity.
    if D > 2:
        theta[2:] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, D - 2)

    m = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.01

    full_start = time.monotonic()
    full_budget = max(1.0, float(deadline - full_start))
    taus = [0.05, 0.035, 0.025, 0.018, 0.012, 0.008, 0.005, 0.003, 0.002]
    fracs = [0.12, 0.13, 0.14, 0.15, 0.17, 0.18, 0.10, 0.06, 0.05]
    # normalize fractions
    s = sum(fracs)
    if s > 0:
        fracs = [f / s for f in fracs]

    boundaries = []
    cum = 0.0
    for f in fracs:
        cum += f * full_budget
        boundaries.append(full_start + cum)

    stage_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stage_idx < len(taus) - 1 and time.monotonic() > boundaries[stage_idx]:
            stage_idx += 1
        tau = taus[stage_idx]

        theta[0] = float(np.clip(theta[0], 0.0, 0.9))
        theta[1] = float(np.clip(theta[1], 0.0, 0.9))
        if D > 2:
            theta[2:] = np.clip(theta[2:], -3.0, 3.0)

        L = basis.dot(theta)
        u = softmax_from_log(L)

        b, maxb = compute_b_scaled(u, n, size1)
        R = 2.0 * maxb

        if R < best_R:
            best_R = R
            best_u = u.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        soft, w = soft_max_weights(b, tau)
        g = cross_grad_scaled(u, w, n, size2)
        h = float(np.dot(g, u))
        gradL = u * (g - h)
        gradtheta = basis.T.dot(gradL)

        if theta[0] <= 0.0:
            gradtheta[0] = 0.0
        elif theta[0] >= 0.9:
            gradtheta[0] = 0.0
        if theta[1] <= 0.0:
            gradtheta[1] = 0.0
        elif theta[1] >= 0.9:
            gradtheta[1] = 0.0

        if D > 2:
            mask = np.abs(theta[2:]) > 3.0
            if np.any(mask):
                g2 = gradtheta[2:].copy()
                g2[mask] = 0.0
                gradtheta[2:] = g2

        theta_new, m, v, t, ok = safe_adam_update(theta, gradtheta, m, v, t, lr)
        if ok:
            theta = theta_new
        it += 1

        if no_improve > 900 and it % 7 == 0:
            theta += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, theta.size)
            if D > 2:
                theta[2:] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, D - 2)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            no_improve = 0

    return best_u, best_R


def run_full(u_in, n, deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1, size2):
    a = normalize_u(u_in.copy())
    L = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    L -= float(np.max(L))
    L = np.clip(L, -60.0, 0.0)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.0032 * float(np.sqrt(8192.0 / float(n)))

    full_start = time.monotonic()
    full_budget = max(1.0, float(deadline - full_start))

    taus = [0.035, 0.025, 0.018, 0.013, 0.009, 0.0065, 0.0045, 0.003, 0.002, 0.0012, 0.0007, 0.00035]
    fracs = [0.03, 0.04, 0.05, 0.06, 0.07, 0.08, 0.10, 0.12, 0.15, 0.13, 0.11, 0.06]
    s = sum(fracs)
    if s > 0:
        fracs = [f / s for f in fracs]

    boundaries = []
    cum = 0.0
    for f in fracs:
        cum += f * full_budget
        boundaries.append(full_start + cum)

    stage_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    print_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stage_idx < len(taus) - 1 and time.monotonic() > boundaries[stage_idx]:
            stage_idx += 1
        tau = taus[stage_idx]

        L -= float(np.max(L))
        L = np.clip(L, -60.0, 0.0)
        u = np.exp(L)
        u = normalize_u(u)

        b, maxb = compute_b_scaled(u, n, size1)
        R = 2.0 * maxb

        if R < best_R:
            best_R = R
            best_u = u.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            print_count += 1
            if print_count % 50 == 1:
                print(f"  full improve R={R:.6f} at t={time.monotonic()-full_start:.1f}s tau={tau:.5f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 1200 and it % 11 == 0:
            sigma = 0.04 + 0.02 * (int(no_improve / 1000) % 4)
            L += lowpass_noise(n, rng, sigma, cutoff=5.0)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            no_improve = 0

            if R > best_R + 0.08:
                a = normalize_u(best_u.copy())
                L = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                L -= float(np.max(L))
                L = np.clip(L, -60.0, 0.0)
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                continue

        soft, w = soft_max_weights(b, tau)
        g = cross_grad_scaled(u, w, n, size2)
        h = float(np.dot(g, u))
        gradL = u * (g - h)

        L_new, m, v, t, ok = safe_adam_update(L, gradL, m, v, t, lr)
        if ok:
            L = L_new
        it += 1

    return best_u, best_R


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    run_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + run_budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Length choice: enforce a power-of-two length that is large but safe.
    if run_budget < 700.0:
        n = 2048
    elif run_budget < 1400.0:
        n = 4096
    elif run_budget < 2050.0:
        n = 8192
    else:
        n = 16384

    L = 2 * n - 1
    size1 = next_pow2(L)
    size2 = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    print(f"[init] n={n}, conv_size={size1}, grad_size={size2}, budget={run_budget:.1f}s")

    cands = generate_initials(n, rng)
    best_u = None
    best_R = np.inf

    for u in cands:
        u = normalize_u(u)
        b, maxb = compute_b_scaled(u, n, size1)
        R = 2.0 * maxb
        if np.isfinite(R) and R < best_R:
            best_R = R
            best_u = u.copy()

    if best_u is None:
        best_u = make_exact_dec(n)
        best_R = 2.0

    print(f"[init] best fast R={best_R:.6f}")

    final_candidates = [
        best_u.copy(),
        make_exact_dec(n),
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    ]

    # Phase 1: fast spectral alternating square-root search.
    remaining = max(1.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    spec_time = min(260.0, 0.13 * remaining)
    spec_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + spec_time)
    if spec_deadline > time.monotonic() + 3.0:
        print(f"[phase] spectral for {spec_time:.1f}s")
        best_u, best_R = run_spectral(best_u, n, spec_deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1)
        final_candidates.append(best_u.copy())
        print(f"[spectral] best fast R={best_R:.6f}")

    # Phase 2: low-dimensional smooth optimization.
    remaining = max(1.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    param_time = min(230.0, 0.12 * remaining)
    param_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + param_time)
    if param_deadline > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        print(f"[phase] parameter/spline for {param_time:.1f}s")
        basis = make_basis(n, 8)
        best_u, best_R = run_parameter(n, basis, param_deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1, size2)
        final_candidates.append(best_u.copy())
        print(f"[parameter] best fast R={best_R:.6f}")

    # Phase 3: full-sequence log-space Adam refinement.
    full_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0))
    if full_deadline > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        print(f"[phase] full refinement until {full_deadline - time.monotonic():.1f}s left")
        best_u, best_R = run_full(best_u, n, full_deadline, best_u, best_R, rng, size1, size2)
        final_candidates.append(best_u.copy())
        print(f"[full] best fast R={best_R:.6f}")

    # Final authoritative evaluation over retained candidates.
    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None

    # Remove exact duplicate references / overly expensive repeats.
    seen = []
    for u in final_candidates:
        if u is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
        arr_norm = normalize_u(arr)
        # Simple early dedup: compare rounded scale and checksum.
        key = (float(np.sum(arr_norm)), int(np.sum((arr_norm * 1e6).astype(np.int64)) & 0xffffffff))
        duped = False
        for old_key in seen:
            if old_key == key:
                duped = True
                break
        seen.append(key)
        if duped:
            continue

        seq = [float(x) for x in arr_norm]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq
        print(f"[final candidate] exact score={sc:.6f}")

    if best_seq is None:
        # Robust fallback: one-sided square-root sequence.
        arr = make_exact_dec(n)
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
        print("[fallback] using exact one-sided square-root sequence")
    else:
        arr = np.maximum(np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            arr *= 1.0 / s
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in arr]

    print(f"[return] n={len(best_seq)}, best known official score={best_score:.6f}")
    return best_seq
```
